```
== 原版规格（调研结论，直接用；不确定处自行回源码核对）==

**Extractinator = tile 219（普通）/ 642（叶绿版 Chlorophyte Extractinator）**。
1. **右键/对准使用**：手持泥沙 Silt(资材 item 999? 核对)/雪泥 Slush/沙漠化石 DesertFossil 对准提取机使用（Player.cs:41034-41049 + :17450 附近）→ 消耗 1 个，产出转化表物品。
2. **1.4.5 新增：通电触发**（Wiring.cs:1994/:2563）——提取机通电时自动消耗其上方/内部的 silt？核对 Wiring.cs Extractinator 分支的真实消耗来源（TileEntity 内置库存？还是检查周围？）——按源码真实语义实现。
3. **转化表**（WorldGen.ExtractinatorUse / Player.cs ExtractinatorUse 一带，约 :41034 起或 WorldGen.cs）：silt/slush → 铜矿/锡矿/银矿/钨矿/金矿/铂金矿/琥珀/钱币 按概率；DesertFossil → 化石套（恐龙装备/琥珀/钱币）；叶绿版 642 的产率差异（核对：更多琥珀/钱币？）。**概率表必须从源码逐项抄录，不许猜**。

== 我方链路（调研结论，直接用）==

- tile 已注册：'v_219_extractinator' / 'v_642_...'（grep src/data/tiles.ts 确认 key）。
- 右键交互入口：Game.ts `interactAt`（约 :3155 起，5 格距离门 :3162）——加分支：手持 silt/slush/化石 时对准提取机 → 转化。手持物判定找 silt/fossil 的 vi_ key（grep items/tiles 数据确认我方物品 key；若 silt 未注册为物品查 VAN() 表）。
- "对准使用物品"路径：updateUse 里也该支持（原版是使用物品而非右键？核对 Player.cs:41034 语义——是 item use 对准 tile，不是右键 interact），若为 use 语义则挂在 updateUse 使用链。
- 通电触发：src/world/wiring/devices.ts deviceTable 加 case 219/642（参考 case 141 炸药的写法与 ctx 接口）；核对原版通电语义后实现。
- 掉落产出：Game.spawnDrop(key, x, y, count)；钱币走 NATIVE_DROP_KEY/coin 体系。
- 粒子/音效：原版转化时的 SoundID 与 dust（核对源码，用现有 sfx/playSfx 占位）。

== 要求 ==
1. 从源码抄录完整转化概率表（两种机型 × 三种输入 × 全产出项），数据表化放新模块 src/world/Extractinator.ts（或并入现有合适模块），每项标行号。
2. 实现 use/interact 两条路径（按原版语义选准）+ 通电触发（devices case）。
3. 消耗逻辑：从玩家物品栏扣 1（对应 Inventory API：inv.removeAt/查找 heldItem）；通电触发的消耗来源按源码。
```

```
	private static void Extractinator(int sourceX, int sourceY)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[sourceX, sourceY];
		int num = sourceX;
		int num2 = sourceY;
		num -= tile.frameX % 54 / 18;
		num2 -= tile.frameY % 54 / 18;
		int time = 60;
		if (!CheckMech(num, num2, time) || !TryFindChestForExtractinator(num, num2, out var chestIndex) || Chest.UsingChest(chestIndex) != -1)
		{
			return;
		}
		int type = tile.type;
		Item[] item = Main.chest[chestIndex].item;
		for (int num3 = Main.chest[chestIndex].maxItems - 1; num3 >= 0; num3--)
		{
			Item item2 = item[num3];
			if (!item2.IsAir)
			{
				ExtractinatorHelper.RollExtractinatorDrop(ItemID.Sets.ExtractinatorMode[item2.type], type, out var itemType, out var stack);
				if (itemType > 0)
				{
					if (--item2.stack <= 0)
					{
						item2.TurnToAir();
					}
					Item.NewItem(new EntitySource_Wiring(num, num2), num * 16, num2 * 16, 32, 32, itemType, stack, noBroadcast: false, -1);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private static bool TryFindChestForExtractinator(int lookupX, int lookupY, out int chestIndex)
	{
		chestIndex = 0;
		int num = 3;
		int num2 = 3;
		int num3 = 2;
		for (int i = lookupX - num3; i <= lookupX + num + num3; i++)
		{
			for (int j = lookupY - num3; j <= lookupY + num2 + num3; j++)
			{
				if (!Chest.IsLocked(i, j))
				{
					int num4 = Chest.FindChest(i, j);
					if (num4 != -1)
					{
						chestIndex = num4;
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool IsHopperInRangeOf(WorldItem item)
	{
		if (ItemID.Sets.ItemsThatShouldNotBeInInventory[item.type])
		{
			return false;
		}
		Rectangle hitbox = item.Hitbox;
		hitbox.Inflate((int)(HopperGrabHitboxSize.X / 2f), (int)(HopperGrabHitboxSize.Y / 2f));
		Point point = hitbox.TopLeft().ToTileCoordinates().ClampedInWorld();
		Point point2 = hitbox.BottomRight().ToTileCoordinates().ClampedInWorld();
		for (int i = point.X; i <= point2.X; i++)
		{
			for (int j = point.Y; j <= point2.Y; j++)
			{
				Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
				if (tile != null && tile.active())
				{
					int type = tile.type;
					if ((type == 21 || type == 467) && tile.anyWire())
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}
```

```
Wiring semantics confirmed:
- Hit wiring case 219/642 → `Extractinator(i,j)`.
- Compute anchor tile: `num = sourceX - tile.frameX % 54 / 18; num2 = sourceY - tile.frameY % 54 / 18;` (extractinator is 3x3, 54x54 px).
- CheckMech(anchor, 60) — 1 second cooldown.
- TryFindChestForExtractinator: search a rectangle from anchor-2 to anchor+3+2 (i.e. lookupX-2 .. lookupX+5, lookupY-2 .. lookupY+5) for a chest (non-locked), FindChest. Wait: num=3, num2=3, num3=2. loop i from lookupX-2 to lookupX+3+2 = +5; j from lookupY-2 to lookupY+3+2 = +5. So chest must be within that box (includes the extractinator tile area and below). Actually the chest is usually placed under the extractinator (fossil material). Chest.UsingChest(chestIndex) != -1 means chest is open by a player → return (don't run).
- Then iterate chest items from last slot backwards; first non-air item, roll. If itemType > 0 (i.e. mode != -1), consume 1 of that item and spawn output as world item. break after first item processed (even if itemType==-1? No — if itemType <= 0 no break, continues to next item). Note: `break` only inside `if (itemType > 0)`.

So powered extractinator consumes from an adjacent chest, not from the extractinator itself.

Now the extractionMode: `ItemID.Sets.ExtractinatorMode[item2.type]`. Need to find which items have which mode. Let me grep for ExtractinatorMode assignments.
```

```
	private void PlaceThing_ItemInExtractinator(ref ItemCheckContext context)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[tileTargetX, tileTargetY];
		Item item = inventory[selectedItem];
		if (!tile.active() || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, item.tileBoost + blockRange) || !ItemTimeIsZero || itemAnimation <= 0 || !controlUseItem)
		{
			return;
		}
		float num = 1f;
		if (tile.type == 642)
		{
			num *= 0.33f;
		}
		ItemTrader itemTrader = TryGettingItemTraderFromBlock(tile);
		if (itemTrader != null && itemTrader.TryGetTradeOption(item, out var option))
		{
			SoundEngine.PlaySound(7);
			ApplyItemTime(item, num);
			context.SkipItemConsumption = true;
			item.stack -= option.TakingItemStack;
			if (item.stack <= 0)
			{
				item.TurnToAir();
			}
			DropItemFromExtractinator(option.GivingItemType, option.GivingItemStack);
		}
		else if (ItemID.Sets.ExtractinatorMode[item.type] >= 0 && (tile.type == 219 || tile.type == 642))
		{
			ApplyItemTime(item, num);
			SoundEngine.PlaySound(7);
			int extractType = ItemID.Sets.ExtractinatorMode[item.type];
			ExtractinatorUse(extractType, tile.type);
		}
	}

	private static ItemTrader TryGettingItemTraderFromBlock(Tile targetBlock)
	{
		ItemTrader result = null;
		if (targetBlock.type == 642)
		{
			result = ItemTrader.ChlorophyteExtractinator;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void PlaceThing_XMasTreeTops()
	{
		if (inventory[selectedItem].type < 1874 || inventory[selectedItem].type > 1905 || !Main.tile[tileTargetX, tileTargetY].active() || Main.tile[tileTargetX, tileTargetY].type != 171 || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, inventory[selectedItem].tileBoost + blockRange) || !ItemTimeIsZero || itemAnimation <= 0 || !controlUseItem)
		{
			return;
		}

	private void ExtractinatorUse(int extractType, int extractinatorBlockType)
	{
		ExtractinatorHelper.RollExtractinatorDrop(extractType, extractinatorBlockType, out var itemType, out var stack);
		if (itemType > 0)
		{
			DropItemFromExtractinator(itemType, stack);
		}
	}

	private void DropItemFromExtractinator(int itemType, int stack)
	{
		Vector2 vector = Main.ReverseGravitySupport(Main.MouseScreen) + Main.screenPosition;
		if (Main.SmartCursorIsUsed || PlayerInput.UsingGamepad)
		{
			vector = base.Center;
		}
		int number = Item.NewItem(GetItemSource_TileInteraction(tileTargetX, tileTargetY), (int)vector.X, (int)vector.Y, 1, 1, itemType, stack, noBroadcast: false, -1);
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(21, -1, -1, null, number, 1f);
		}
	}

	public void ChangeDir(int dir)
	{
		if (dir == direction)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (pulley && pulleyDir == 2)
		{
			int num = (int)(position.X + (float)(width / 2)) / 16 * 16 + 8 - width / 2;
			if (Collision.SolidCollision(new Vector2(num, position.Y), width, height))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (whoAmI == Main.myPlayer)
			{
				Main.cameraX = Main.cameraX + position.X - (float)num;
			}
			pulleyDir = 1;
			position.X = num;
		}
		direction = dir;
		compositeBackArm.rotation *= -1f;
		compositeFrontArm.rotation *= -1f;
		itemRotation *= -1f;
		itemLocation.X = MountedCenter.X + (MountedCenter.X - itemLocation.X);
	}

	public Rectangle getRect()
	{
		return new Rectangle((int)position.X, (int)position.Y, width, height);
	}
```

```
				else if (type == 209 && Main.tile[tX, tY].frameX / 72 < 3 && IsInTileInteractionRange(tX, tY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, cannonRange))

					flag = true;
				}
				else if (Main.tileHammer[type] && IsInTileInteractionRange(tX, tY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, hammerRange))
				{
					toolStrategy = 1;
					flag = true;

				else if (Main.tileAxe[type] && IsInTileInteractionRange(tX, tY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, axeRange))
				{
					toolStrategy = 2;
					flag = true;

				else if (type == 184 && IsInTileInteractionRange(tX, tY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, paintScraperRange))
				{
					toolStrategy = 8;
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (IsInTileInteractionRange(tX, tY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, pickRange))
				{
					toolStrategy = 3;
					flag = true;

				toolStrategy = 4;
				flag = true;

		}
		if (!flag && wet)

	private void SmartSelect_GetAvailableToolRanges(out int pickRange, out int axeRange, out int hammerRange, out int cannonRange, out int extractItemRange, out int paintScraperRange)
	{
		pickRange = -10;
		axeRange = -10;

				pickRange = inventory[i].tileBoost;

			if (inventory[i].axe > 0 && axeRange == -10)
			{
				axeRange = inventory[i].tileBoost;
			}
			if (inventory[i].hammer > 0 && hammerRange == -10)
			{
				hammerRange = inventory[i].tileBoost;

			if (ItemID.Sets.Torches[inventory[i].type] && cannonRange == -10)
			{
				cannonRange = inventory[i].tileBoost;
			}
			if (ItemID.Sets.IsPaintScraper[inventory[i].type] && paintScraperRange == -10)
			{
				paintScraperRange = inventory[i].tileBoost;
			}
			if (ItemID.Sets.ExtractinatorMode[inventory[i].type] != -1 && extractItemRange == -10)
			{
				extractItemRange = inventory[i].tileBoost;
			}
		}
	}
```

```
	private bool GetItem_FillEmptyInventorySlot(Item newItem, GetItemSettings settings, Item returnItem, int i)
	{
		if (inventory[i].type != 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!settings.NoSound)
		{
			SoundEngine.PlaySound(newItem.IsACoin ? 38 : 7);

		inventory[i] = returnItem;

		}
		if (!settings.NoCoinMerge)
		{
			DoCoins(i);
		}
		AchievementsHelper.NotifyItemPickup(this, returnItem);

		{
			LucyAxeMessage.Create(LucyAxeMessage.MessageSource.PickedUp, base.Top, new Vector2(0f, -7f));
		}
		settings.HandlePostAction(inventory[i]);
		return true;
	}
```

```
3537:	public const short Amber = 999;
4023:	public const short AmberMosquito = 1242;
8299:	public const short FossilOre = 3380;
1681:	public const short CopperCoin = 71;
1903:	public const short Diamond = 182;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using ReLogic.Reflection;
using Terraria.DataStructures;
using Terraria.GameContent.Items;

namespace Terraria.ID;

public class ItemID
{
	public struct BannerEffect
	{
		public static readonly BannerEffect None = new BannerEffect(0f);

		public static readonly BannerEffect Reduced = new BannerEffect(0.2f);

		public readonly GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve DamageDealt;

		public readonly GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve DamageReceived;

		public readonly bool Enabled;

		public BannerEffect(float strength = 1f)
		{
			float output = 1f + strength * 0.5f;
			float output2 = 1f + strength;
			DamageDealt = new GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve(new GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, output), new GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, output2));
			float num = 1f / (strength + 1f);
			float output3 = (1f + num) / 2f;
			DamageReceived = new GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve(new GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, output3), new GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, num));
			Enabled = strength != 0f;
		}

		public BannerEffect(GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve damageDealt, GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve damageReceived)
		{
			DamageDealt = damageDealt;
			DamageReceived = damageReceived;
			Enabled = true;
		}
	}

```

```
  countProjectiles(kind: 'spiky' | 'bunny', px: number, py: number, radius: number): number;

  doorOpen(x: number, y: number): boolean;
  doorClose(x: number, y: number): boolean;

  teleportPair(a: [number, number], b: [number, number]): void;
  /** 城镇 NPC 搬迁雕像(style 40/41):group 0/1 白名单,搬到 (px,py) */
  relocateTownNpc(group: 0 | 1, px: number, py: number): boolean;
  /** 爆炸(炸药/地雷/炮弹):范围伤害+清格+粒子 */

const GEMSPARK_OFF = ['v_255_amethystgemsparkoff', 'v_256_topazgemsparkoff', 'v_257_sapphiregemsparkoff',
  'v_258_emeraldgemsparkoff', 'v_259_rubygemsparkoff', 'v_260_diamondgemsparkoff', 'v_261_ambergemsparkoff'];
const GEMSPARK_ON = ['v_262_amethystgemspark', 'v_263_topazgemspark', 'v_264_sapphiregemspark',
  'v_265_emeraldgemspark', 'v_266_rubygemspark', 'v_267_diamondgemspark', 'v_268_ambergemspark'];

/** 通电石/宝石块等的成对 tile id 切换 */
function swapTile(w: Wiring, x: number, y: number, fromKey: string, toKey: string): void {
  const st = w.st;
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const cur = st.type[i];
  const from = TILE_BY_KEY[fromKey], to = TILE_BY_KEY[toKey];
  if (cur === from) st.setTile(x, y, to, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
  else if (cur === to) st.setTile(x, y, from, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
}

/** 多格器件整体翻帧(篝火/壁炉/吊灯等通用模式,Wiring.cs:1003-1052 等):
 *  锚点 = (x - frameX%blockW/18, y - frameY%blockH/18);delta 由锚点帧阈值定;整体 SkipWire */
function flipBlock(
  w: Wiring, x: number, y: number,
  blockW: number, blockH: number, tilesW: number, tilesH: number,
  axis: 'x' | 'y', threshold: number, delta: number,
): void {
  const st = w.st;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % blockW / 18);
  const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % blockH / 18);
  let d = delta;
  const ai = st.idx(ox, oy);
  if (axis === 'y' ? st.frameY[ai] >= threshold : st.frameX[ai] >= threshold) d = -delta;
  for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + tilesW; ix++) {
    for (let iy = oy; iy < oy + tilesH; iy++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
      const ii = st.idx(ix, iy);
      w.skipCell(ix, iy);
      if (axis === 'y') st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii], st.frameY[ii] + d);
      else st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii] + d, st.frameY[ii]);
    }
  }
}

/** 单格帧翻转(火把/蜡烛等) */
```

```
/** HitWireSingle 器件主分发表。返回 true = 已处理 */
export function deviceTable(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, sheet: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const st = w.st;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  switch (sheet) {
    case 4: // 火把 frameX ±66(:1207-1213)
      flipCell(w, x, y, 66);
      return true;
    case 10: // 关门 → 开(随机方向+反向,原版 :1171-1188)
      ctx.doorOpen(x, y);
      return true;
    case 11:
      ctx.doorClose(x, y);
      return true;
    case 15: { // 马桶(frameY/40 判定,:1194-1204;喷水近似粒子)
      if (Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 1 && Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 20) return true;
      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 60)) ctx.sfx('splash', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
      return true;
    }
    case 33: case 174: case 49: case 372: // 蜡烛族 frameX ±18(:1472-1480)
      flipCell(w, x, y, 18);
      return true;
    case 34: // 吊灯 3×3 frameX ±54(:1405-1428)
      flipBlock(w, x, y, 108, 54, 3, 3, 'x', 54, 54);
      return true;
    case 35: case 139: case 207: case 410: case 480: case 509:
      // 南瓜灯/八音盒/喷泉/三色天塔柱:原版 SwitchMB/SwitchFountain/SwitchMonolith。
      // 引擎暂无对应状态系统 → toast 占位
      ctx.toast('装置切换(音乐/喷泉/天塔柱系统未接入)');
      return true;
    case 42: // 挂链灯笼 1×2 frameX ±18(:1214-1227)
      flipBlock(w, x, y, 36, 36, 1, 2, 'x', 18, 18);
      return true;
    case 92: // 路灯柱 1×6 frameX ±18(:1483-1494)
      flipBlock(w, x, y, 18, 108, 1, 6, 'x', 18, 18);
      return true;
    case 93: // 蒂基火炬 1×3 frameX ±18(:1228-1243)
      flipBlock(w, x, y, 18, 54, 1, 3, 'x', 18, 18);
      return true;
    case 95: case 100: case 126: case 173: case 564: // 2×2 灯具族 frameX ±36(:1381-1404)
      flipBlock(w, x, y, 72, 36, 2, 2, 'x', 36, 36);
      return true;
    case 105:
      fireStatue(w, ctx, x, y);
      return true;
    case 130: { // 通电石:上方有宝箱则不缩(:878-884)
      const above = y > 0 ? w.sheetOf(x, y - 1) : -1;
      if (above === 21 || above === 88 || above === 467) return true;
      swapTile(w, x, y, 'v_130_active_stone_block', 'v_131_inactivestoneblock');
      return true;
    }
    case 131:
      swapTile(w, x, y, 'v_130_active_stone_block', 'v_131_inactivestoneblock');
      return true;
    case 137:
      fireTrap(w, ctx, x, y);
      return true;
    case 141: // 炸药(:1781-1785)：清格 + Projectile 108(500伤/半径10)
      w.skipCell(x, y);
      st.setTile(x, y, 0);
      ctx.explode(x, y, 500, 10);
      return true;
    case 149: // 节日彩灯 frameX ±54(:1244-1250)
      flipCell(w, x, y, 54);
      return true;
    case 209: { // 大炮（Wiring.cs:1237-1346 1:1）：旋转+开火复合状态机。
      // num31=块内列(0/3=旋转态,1/2=待发) num32=行内偏移 num35=仰角带(frameY/54,0-8)
      // num36=水平带(frameX/72: 0/1=右向 大炮/兔兔炮, 3/4=左向)
      const num31 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 72 / 18);
      const num32 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
      const ox = x - num31, oy = y - num32;
      const num35 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 54);
      const num36 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 72);
      let num37 = -1;
      if (num31 === 1 || num31 === 2) num37 = num32;
      // 旋转半边：列3 → 仰角 -54（带0停）；列0 → +54（带8停）
      let num38 = 0;
      if (num31 === 3) num38 = -54;
      if (num31 === 0) num38 = 54;
      if (num35 >= 8 && num38 > 0) num38 = 0;
      if (num35 === 0 && num38 < 0) num38 = 0;
      if (num38 !== 0) {
        for (let cx = ox; cx < ox + 4; cx++) {
          for (let cy = oy; cy < oy + 3; cy++) {
            if (!st.inBounds(cx, cy)) continue;
            w.skipCell(cx, cy);
            const ci = st.idx(cx, cy);
            st.setTile(cx, cy, st.type[ci], st.frameX[ci], st.frameY[ci] + num38);
          }
        }
      }
      // 水平朝向带翻转：3/4 带 + 行0/1 → frameX ±72（下一次触发反向）
      if ((num36 === 3 || num36 === 4) && (num37 === 0 || num37 === 1)) {
        const fx = num36 === 3 ? 72 : -72;
        for (let cx = ox; cx < ox + 4; cx++) {
          for (let cy = oy; cy < oy + 3; cy++) {

      if (num36 === 1 && w.bunnyCannonCoolDown > 0) return true;
```

```
// 物品注册表
export type ToolType = 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer';
// Buff 类型（药水饮用后生效）
export type PotionBuff = 'agility' | 'ironskin' | 'resistance' | 'thorns' | 'regen'
  | 'obsidianSkin' | 'gills' | 'manaRegen' | 'magicPower' | 'featherfall' | 'waterWalking' | 'archery' | 'nightOwl';
// 盔甲部位：0 头盔 1 胸甲 2 胫甲（与 Inventory.armorSlotReq 的格位对应）
export type ArmorSlot = 0 | 1 | 2;

export interface ItemDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  maxStack: number;
  tile?: string;          // 放置的 tile key
  placeStyle?: number;     // 放置的样式索引(vanilla style 帧 X 偏移 = style*fw*18)
  magic?: {               // 魔法武器:消耗 mana 发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    manaCost: number;
    projSpeed: number;
    proj?: 'spark' | 'bolt'; // 投射物外观
  };
  ranged?: {              // 远程武器:消耗 ammo 弹药发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    knockback: number;
    ammo: string;         // 弹药 item key(箭)
    projSpeed: number;    // 投射物速度 px/tick
    /** 原版 item id（PickAmmo/ItemCheck_Shoot 数值以 vanilla-itemcombat.json 为准） */
    vid?: number;
  };
  /** 原版 AmmoID（40=箭；弹药物品自身标识，PickAmmo 匹配弓 useAmmo） */
  ammoId?: number;
  /** 原版 item id（战斗数值数据源） */
  vid?: number;
  wall?: string;          // 放置的 wall key
  /** 原版 createWall（Item.cs SetDefaults）：放置的背景墙 vanilla id。
   *  vanilla-wallitems.json 全量 124 项（tools/extract-wallitems.mjs 提取） */
  wallId?: number;
  tool?: {
    type: ToolType;
    power?: number;       // 工具力（镐/斧/锤）
    damage?: number;      // 武器伤害
    speed?: number;       // 挥砍动画 tick（≈原版 useAnimation）
    /** 原版 useTime（Item.cs SetDefaults）：挖掘每击冷却——原版挖掘节奏的真基准，
     *  铜 15-23 / 铁 13-20 / 银 11-19 / 金 17-23（applyItemTime 冷却=useTime） */
    useTime?: number;
    knockback?: number;
    reach?: number;       // 攻击/作用半径（px）
  };
  /** 副工具力（Hamaxe 系双工具：主 tool.type 外的另一力，挖掘入口按主类型判定） */
  axePower?: number;
  heal?: number;          // 食用/饮用回复
  potion?: {
    buff: PotionBuff;
    duration: number;     // 秒
    isHealType: boolean;  // 受"耐药性"封锁
  };
  accessory?: 'lucky_horseshoe' | 'feral_claws'; // 配饰效果
  armor?: { slot: ArmorSlot; defense: number };   // 盔甲（可穿装备/时装格，仅装备格计防御）
  value?: number;         // 钱币价值（铜币）
  wireTool?: {            // 电路工具（原版 Player.cs:30289-30444 ItemCheck_UseWiringTools）
    place?: number;       // 单击放置的导线位掩码(TOOL_RED/BLUE/GREEN/YELLOW/ACTUATOR)
    cutter?: boolean;     // 剪线钳:按优先级移除一件(致动器>黄>绿>蓝>红)
    rod?: boolean;        // 致动魔杖:手动翻转致动状态
    grand?: boolean;      // 宏伟蓝图:拖拽批量(R 键切模式)
  };
  desc?: string;
}

export const ITEM_DEFS: ItemDef[] = [];
const byKey: Record<string, number> = {};
export const ITEM_BY_KEY: Record<string, number> = byKey;

function item(d: Partial<ItemDef> & { key: string; name: string }) {
  byKey[d.key] = ITEM_DEFS.length;
  ITEM_DEFS.push({ maxStack: 999, ...d } as ItemDef);
}

// ---- 基础方块 ----
item({ key: 'dirt_block', name: '泥土块', tile: 'dirt' });
...
item({ key: 'vi_3511_silver_hammer', name: '', maxStack: 999, value: 1 });
item({ key: 'vi_3517_gold_hammer', name: '', maxStack: 999, value: 1 });
item({ key: 'vi_3525_stardust_hamaxe', name: '', maxStack: 999, value: 1 });
item({ key: 'vi_4317_haemorrhaxe', name: '', maxStack: 999, value: 1 });

// 月事件触发物品（Player.cs:43294/43361：夜晚 && 无进行中月事件；Main.startPumpkinMoon/

      }
```

```
      this.toggleDoor(tx, ty, false);
    } else if (type === T.CHEST || this.wiring?.sheetOf(tx, ty) === 441 || this.wiring?.sheetOf(tx, ty) === 468) {
      // 陷阱箱 441/468 也走开箱流程(tryOpenChest 内触发电路+射镖)
      // 打开宝箱：精确命中或 3×3 容差（点击宝箱边缘也算）
      const chest = this.findChest(tx, ty) ?? this.findChestNear(tx, ty);
      if (chest) this.tryOpenChest(chest);
      else if (this.net) {
        // 联机：本地无箱数据（msg7 不传 chests，权威在服务器）→ msg31 请求，
        // onChestSync 回包后登记 world.chests 并自动开面板
        this.netPendingChest = { x: tx, y: ty };
        this.net.requestChestOpen(tx, ty);
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      }
    } else if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['tombstone_v']) {
      // 墓碑：读碑文（原版 Sign 阅读；碑文在 2×2 锚点登记，点任意一格都能读到）
      const sign = this.world.signs.find((s) =>
        tx >= s.x && tx <= s.x + 1 && ty >= s.y && ty <= s.y + 1);
      if (sign) this.cb.onReadSign?.(sign.text);
      this.input.rightDown = false;
    } else if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['crystal_heart']) {
      // 生命水晶(放置态):右键使用(Player.cs ItemCheck_UseLifeCrystal L29358)
      // statLifeMax<400 → +20 上限并回满该部分,放置物消耗
      this.usePlacedLifeCrystal(tx, ty);
    } else if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['bed']) {
      // 床:设重生点(vanilla Player.FindBed 语义;敌怪环绕检查略)
      this.world.spawnX = tx + 1;
      this.world.spawnY = ty - 1;
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SpawnSet'));
      this.input.rightDown = false;
    } else if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_125_crystal_ball'] || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_354_bewitching_table']
      || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_377_sharpening_station'] || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_287_ammo_box']
      || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_464_war_table'] || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_621_slice_of_cake']) {
      // 工位 buff（Player.cs:32126-32162 右键授予）：水晶球 29(1800s)/附魔台 150/磨刀石 159/
      // 弹药箱 93/战争桌 348 均 108000t=1800s；蛋糕 192=7200t=120s
      const dur = type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_621_slice_of_cake'] ? 120 : 1800;
      const bt = type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_125_crystal_ball'] ? BuffType.Clairvoyance
        : type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_354_bewitching_table'] ? BuffType.Bewitched
          : type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_377_sharpening_station'] ? BuffType.Sharpened
            : type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_287_ammo_box'] ? BuffType.AmmoBox
              : type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_464_war_table'] ? BuffType.WarTable : BuffType.SugarRush;
      this.player.buffs.apply(bt, dur);
      this.sfx.play('drink'); // 原版 SoundID.Item4（药水/授予类 UseSound 占位）
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
      this.input.rightDown = false;
    } else if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_132_lever'] || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_136_switch'] || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_144_timers']
      || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_411_detonator']) {
      // 拉杆/开关/计时器:切换通断(帧 +18 翻转),触发直线可见的陷阱(WorldGen Wiring 简化)
      // 起爆器 411：原版为"对准使用任意物品"触发（Player.cs:31865）+ 坠落触发（:34368，
      // 已接 Game.fixedUpdate landImpactVy）——本作并入右键交互
      this.toggleSwitch(tx, ty, type);
    } else if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_83_herbs_mature'] || type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_84_herbs_bloom']) {
      // 药草收获:成熟/开花态右键 → 掉种子(style = frameX/18,WorldGen.cs:38731)
      const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
      const style = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
      st.setTile(tx, ty, 0);
      const seedKeys = ['vi_307_daybloom_seeds', 'vi_308_moonglow_seeds', 'vi_309_blinkroot_seeds',
        'vi_311_waterleaf_seeds', 'vi_310_deathweed_seeds', 'vi_312_fireblossom_seeds', 'vi_2357_shiverthorn_seeds'];
      const sk = seedKeys[style] ?? seedKeys[0];
      this.spawnDrop(tx * 16 + 8, ty * 16, sk, 1);
      this.input.rightDown = false;
    }
  }

  /** 放置态生命水晶使用(Player.cs:29358:statLifeMax≥400 拒绝,+20 上限+回复,消耗) */
  private usePlacedLifeCrystal(tx: number, ty: number): void {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const def = TILE_DEFS[st.get(tx, ty)];
    if (this.player.baseMaxHp >= 400) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.LifeMaxReached'));
      this.input.rightDown = false;
      return;
    }
    this.player.baseMaxHp += 20;
    this.player.hp = Math.min(this.player.maxHp, this.player.hp + 20);
    this.addDamageNumber(this.player.cx, this.player.y, 20, false, '#40E080');
    // 消耗放置物(2×2)
    for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
      for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
        if (st.get(tx + dx, ty + dy) === TILE_BY_KEY['crystal_heart']) st.setTile(tx + dx, ty + dy, 0);
      }
    }
    this.sfx.play('drink');
    this.input.rightDown = false;
    void def;
  }

  /** 开宝箱统一入口:锁定箱(原版 Chest.locked)需金钥匙,首次开启消耗 */
  private tryOpenChest(chest: { locked?: boolean }): void {
    // 物品保护（v3 房间制）：非房主禁开宝箱（v1 无箱子内容同步，取放全在本地——门禁开箱即拦截）
    if (!this.netCanChestInteract()) { this.netDeny(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.RoomProtectItems')); return; }
    if (chest.locked) {
      const keyId = ITEM_BY_KEY['vi_327_golden_key'];
      let keySlot = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < this.player.inv.slots.length; i++) {
        const sl = this.player.inv.slots[i];
        if (sl && sl.id === keyId && sl.stack > 0) { keySlot = i; break; }
      }
      if (keySlot < 0) { this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.ChestLocked')); this.input.rightDown = false; return; }
      this.player.inv.removeAt(keySlot, 1);
      chest.locked = false;
      this.sfx.play('tink');
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.ChestUnlocked'));
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    }
    // 陷阱箱 441/468(Player.cs:21303 语义):开启即触发电路 + 向玩家八方射镖
    const cxy = chest as { x?: number; y?: number };
    const csh = cxy.x !== undefined && cxy.y !== undefined ? this.wiring?.sheetOf(cxy.x, cxy.y) ?? -1 : -1;
    if ((csh === 441 || csh === 468) && cxy.x !== undefined && cxy.y !== undefined) {
      this.wiring?.hitSwitch(cxy.x, cxy.y);
      const cxp = cxy.x * TILE + 16, cyp = cxy.y * TILE + 16;
      for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
        const ang = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
        this.entities.add(new TrapShot(cxp, cyp, Math.cos(ang) * 6, Math.sin(ang) * 6, 20, 'dart'), 'projectiles');
      }
      this.sfx.play('tink');
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.ChestTrapped'));
    }
    this.openChest?.(chest as never);
    this.input.rightDown = false;
  }

  /** 拉杆/开关/计时器:右键 → Wiring.HitSwitch(Wiring.cs:163-252) */
  private toggleSwitch(x: number, y: number, type: number): void {
    void type;
    this.wiring?.hitSwitch(x, y);
    this.sfx.play('tink');
    this.input.rightDown = false;
  }

  /** 压力板 135:踩上瞬间 HitSwitch(Collision.SwitchTiles 玩家语义;敌人触发见 Enemy 侧) */

```

```
  private updateUse(dt: number) {
    const inp = this.input;

    if (this.minecart) {

      if (ix !== 0) cart.vx = Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, cart.vx + ix * 0.15));
      if (inp.down('KeyR')) {
        // 跳车(原版 R 键下车;Space 与跳跃共用会误触)
        this.player.x = cart.x;
        this.player.y = cart.y - 24;
        this.player.vy = -4;
        cart.mounted = false;
        this.minecart = null;
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      }
      // 玩家跟随车位置
      this.player.x = cart.x;
      this.player.y = cart.y - 26;
      this.player.vx = cart.vx;
      this.player.vy = 0;
      if (cart.dead) { this.minecart = null; }
      return;
    }
    // 全屏地图打开时鼠标交给地图（拖动/缩放/传送），不触发挖掘/放置/攻击/使用。

      this.mining = null;
      return;
    }
    const held = this.player.inv.heldItem();
    const heldDef = held ? ITEM_DEFS[held.id] : null;
    const [rawTx, rawTy] = this.camera.tileUnder(inp.mouseX, inp.mouseY);
    let tx = rawTx, ty = rawTy;
    // 工具判定：legacy def.tool 优先；vi_ 镐/斧/锤经 vanilla-itemfunc 桥接（数值取 combat 表）

        const cart = new Minecart();
        cart.x = tx * TILE; cart.y = (ty - 1) * TILE;
        this.entities.add(cart, 'props');
        this.minecart = cart;
        cart.mounted = true;
        this.player.inv.removeAt(this.player.inv.selected, 1);
        this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
        this.input.rightDown = false;
        return;
      }
    }
    if (inp.rightDown) {
      const nearChest = this.findChestNear(tx, ty);
      if (nearChest) {
        this.tryOpenChest(nearChest);
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      } else if (this.talkToNearbyNpc(tx, ty)) {
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      } else if (heldDef?.key === 'suspicious_eye') {
        if (!this.world.clock.isDay) {

          this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
        } else {
          this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NothingHappened'));
        }
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      } else {
        this.interactAt(tx, ty);
      }
    }
```

```
  private makeDeviceCtx() {
    return {
      worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel,
      dropItem: (vanillaItemId: number, px: number, py: number) => {
        const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaItemId];
        if (!key) return;
        const d = this.spawnDrop(px, py, key, 1);
        // 心/星雕像是 pickup 类物品（ItemID.Sets.IsAPickup）
        if (d && vanillaItemId === 58) d.pickup = 'heart';
        else if (d && vanillaItemId === 184) d.pickup = 'star';
      },
      spawnNpc: (vanillaNpcId: number, px: number, py: number) => {
        const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(vanillaNpcId, px, py);
        if (e) {
          // Wiring.cs:2479-2483：雕像产怪 value=0（不掉钱）+ SpawnedFromStatue
          e.spawnedFromStatue = true;
          this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      },
      mechSpawnOk: (px: number, py: number, type: number) => {
        // NPC.MechSpawn（NPC.cs:7399-7483）：同 type（等价组互计）三阈值拒绝
        const grp = NPC_MECH_GROUPS.find((g) => g.includes(type));
        let total = 0, near = 0, mid = 0;
        for (const o of this.entities.enemies) {
          const en = o as Enemy;
          const t = en.vanillaId;
          if (t == null) continue;
          if (t !== type && !(grp && grp.includes(t)) && !(type === 55 && t === 230)) continue;
          total++;
          const d = Math.hypot(en.x - px, en.y - py);
          if (d < 200) near++;
          if (d < 600) mid++;
          if (near >= 3 || mid >= 6 || total >= 10) return false;
        }
        return true;
      },
      itemSpawnOk: (px: number, py: number, type: number) => {
        // Item.MechSpawn（Item.cs:48982-49013）：同 item 三阈值拒绝
        let total = 0, near = 0, mid = 0;
        for (const o of this.entities.drops) {
          const d = o as ItemDrop;
          if (dropVanillaId(d) !== type) continue;
          total++;
          const dist = Math.hypot(d.x - px, d.y - py);
          if (dist < 300) near++;
          if (dist < 800) mid++;
          if (near >= 3 || mid >= 6 || total >= 10) return false;
        }
        return true;
      },
      shootProjectile: (px: number, py: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, kind: TrapShotKind) => {
        this.entities.add(new TrapShot(px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind), 'projectiles');
        // 发射音：陷阱族=敲石近似；炮弹族由 TrapShot 首帧按原版播 Item14（Projectile.cs:21542/26830）
        if (kind !== 'cannonball' && kind !== 'bunny' && kind !== 'snowball') this.sfxAt('tink', px, py);
      },
      countProjectiles: (kind: 'spiky' | 'bunny', px: number, py: number, radius: number) => {
        let n = 0;
        for (const e of this.entities.projectiles) {
          if (e instanceof TrapShot && e.kind === kind && Math.hypot(e.x - px, e.y - py) < radius) n++;
        }
        return n;
      },
      doorOpen: (x: number, y: number) => {
        // 原版 HitWireSingle 10/11:随机方向 + 反向重试(WorldGen.OpenDoor)
        const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
        if (!openDoor(this.world.store, x, y, dir)) return openDoor(this.world.store, x, y, -dir) ;
        return true;
      },
      doorClose: (x: number, y: number) => closeDoor(this.world.store, x, y),
      teleportPair: (a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) => this.teleportPairImpl(a, b),
      relocateTownNpc: (group: 0 | 1, px: number, py: number) => {
        // 雕像 40/41 城镇 NPC 白名单(原版两组;此处按 NPC 图鉴顺序取前几个存在的)
        const groups: Array<Array<string>> = [
          ['guide', 'merchant', 'nurse', 'demolitionist', 'dyer', 'arms_dealer'],
          ['dryad', 'painter', 'golfer', 'zoologist', 'pirate', 'stylist'],
        ];
        for (const key of groups[group]) {
          const npc = this.entities.npcs.find((n) => (n as TownNPC).npcKey === key);
          if (npc) {
            npc.x = px - npc.w / 2;
            npc.y = py - npc.h - 1;
            return true;
          }
        }
        return false;
      },
      explode: (tx: number, ty: number, damage: number, radius?: number, destroyTiles?: boolean) =>
        this.explodeAt(tx, ty, damage, radius, destroyTiles),
      toast: (m: string) => this.cb.onToast(m),
      sfx: (n: string, x?: number, y?: number) => {
        if (x !== undefined && y !== undefined) this.sfxAt(n as SfxName, x, y);
        else this.sfx.play(n as SfxName);
      },
    };
  }

  /** 传送器对(Wiring.cs:2314-2365):板上 48×48 判定框内实体双向搬迁 */
  private teleportPairImpl(a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) {
    // 太近的竖叠板拒绝(:2316)
    if (Math.abs(a[0] - b[0]) < 3 && a[1] > b[1] - 3 && a[1] < b[1]) return;
    const rect = (pad: [number, number]) => ({
      x: pad[0] * TILE, y: pad[1] * TILE - 48, w: 48, h: 48,
    });
    const hit = (r: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
      e.x < r.x + r.w && e.x + e.w > r.x && e.y < r.y + r.h && e.y + e.h > r.y;
    const ra = rect(a), rb = rect(b);
    const dx = rb.x - ra.x, dy = rb.y - ra.y;
    if (!this.wiring?.blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration) {
      const p = this.player;
      if (!p.dead && hit(ra, p)) { p.x += dx; p.y += dy; p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0; this.sfx.play('tink'); }
      else if (!p.dead && hit(rb, p)) { p.x -= dx; p.y -= dy; p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0; this.sfx.play('tink'); }
    }
    for (const e of this.entities.enemies) {
      if (hit(ra, e)) { e.x += dx; e.y += dy; }
      else if (hit(rb, e)) { e.x -= dx; e.y -= dy; }
    }
  }

  /** 爆炸(炸药/地雷/巨石近似):半径 3 清软块 + 范围伤害 + 粒子 */
  /** 爆炸不可炸 tile 表（Projectile.CanExploteTile cs:75764 1:1）：
   *  地牢砖族 tileDungeon{41,43,44,677-679}(Main.cs:7941) + 基础宝箱 BasicChest{21,467}
   *  (TileID.cs:315) + switch 黑名单{26 祭坛,88 柜子,121/122 史莱姆块?,150 蛛网? ,
   *  211 血月砖?,226 蜥蜴砖,237 蜥蜴祭坛,248-250 尖刺?,346/470/475/504 陷阱族,685/686} */
  private static readonly EXPLODE_NEVER = new Set([41, 43, 44, 677, 678, 679, 21, 467,
    26, 88, 121, 122, 150, 211, 226, 237, 248, 249, 250, 346, 470, 475, 504, 685, 686]);
  /** 困难模式矿石（仅 scarab 弹 1086/1087 可炸——未引入恒 false） */
  private static readonly EXPLODE_HM_ORE = new Set([107, 108, 111, 221, 223]);
  /** 爆炸半径表（Projectile.cs:75262）：炸弹族=4、炸药棍族=7、放置炸药 108=10；
   *  表外（手雷 30 等）= 不破坏地形（Kill 判定表 :75260 之外） */
  static readonly EXPLODE_RADIUS: Record<number, number> = {
    28: 4, 37: 4, 516: 4, 519: 4, 29: 7, 470: 7, 637: 7, 108: 10,
  };
  /** CanExploteTile 纯函数版（测试用）：规则见 explodeAt 注释 */
  static canExplodeTile(
    st: World['store'], x: number, y: number, hardMode: boolean, golemDown: boolean, lavaLine: number,
  ): boolean {
    const t = st.get(x, y);
    const sheet = TILE_DEFS[t]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
    if (Game.EXPLODE_NEVER.has(sheet)) return false;
    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
    if (st.wall[ii] === 350) return false;
    if (Game.EXPLODE_HM_ORE.has(sheet)) return false;
    if ((sheet === 37 || sheet === 58) && !hardMode) return false;
    if (sheet === 77 && !hardMode && y >= lavaLine) return false;
    if (sheet === 137 && !golemDown) {
      const row = Math.floor(st.frameY[ii] / 18);
      if (row >= 1 && row <= 4) return false; // 神庙门行（:75805 (uint)(num-1)<=3）
    }
```

```
  private p2i(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.st.w + x; }
  private i2x(i: number): number { return i % this.st.w; }
  private i2y(i: number): number { return (i / this.st.w) | 0; }
  private sheetAt(x: number, y: number): number { return this.sheetOf(x, y); }
  /** 该格的原版 tile id(sheet;非 vanilla tile 返回 -1)。器件表按 sheet 分发 */
  sheetOf(x: number, y: number): number {
    if (!this.st.inBounds(x, y)) return -1;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.st.type[this.p2i(x, y)]];
    return d?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
  }
  /** 多格器件整体去重(devices.ts 用) */
  skipCell(x: number, y: number): void { this.skipWire(x, y); }

  // ============ UpdateMech(Wiring.cs:88-161)每 tick 调 ============

  updateMech(): void {
    // 炮族全局冷却每 tick 递减（Wiring.cs:147-158）
    if (this.cannonCoolDown > 0) this.cannonCoolDown--;
    if (this.bunnyCannonCoolDown > 0) this.bunnyCannonCoolDown--;
    if (this.snowballCannonCoolDown > 0) this.snowballCannonCoolDown--;
    for (let i = this.numMechs - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      this.mechTime[i]--;
      const mx = this.mechX[i], my = this.mechY[i];
      const sh = this.sheetAt(mx, my);
      const raw = this.st.type[this.p2i(mx, my)] !== 0;
      if (raw && sh === 144) {
        const ti = this.p2i(mx, my);
        if (this.st.frameY[ti] === 0) {
          this.mechTime[i] = 0;
        } else {
          // frameX/18 → 周期帧数(:102-120)
          let period = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18);
          period = period === 0 ? 60 : period === 1 ? 180 : period === 2 ? 300 : period === 3 ? 30 : period === 4 ? 15 : 60;
          // IEEERemainder(t, period) === 0(注意负数:IEEERemainder 结果范围 (-p,p])
          const r = this.mechTime[i] - period * Math.round(this.mechTime[i] / period);
          if (r === 0) {
            this.mechTime[i] = 18000;
            this.tripWire(mx, my, 1, 1);
          }
        }
      }
      if (this.mechTime[i] <= 0) {
        if (raw && sh === 144) {
          const ti = this.p2i(mx, my);
          this.st.setTile(mx, my, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], 0);
        }
        if (raw && sh === 411) {
          // 引爆器 2×2 帧复位(:135-151)
          const ti = this.p2i(mx, my);
          const num1 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] % 36 / 18);
          const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] % 36 / 18);
          const tileX = mx - num1, tileY = my - num2;
          let num3 = 36;
          if (this.st.frameX[this.p2i(tileX, tileY)] >= 36) num3 = -36;
          for (let ix = tileX; ix < tileX + 2; ix++) {
            for (let iy = tileY; iy < tileY + 2; iy++) {
              const ii = this.p2i(ix, iy);
              if (this.sheetAt(ix, iy) === 411) this.st.setTile(ix, iy, this.st.type[ii], this.st.frameX[ii] + num3, this.st.frameY[ii]);
            }
          }
        }
        // 数组下移移除(:152-158)
        for (let k = i; k < this.numMechs; k++) {
          this.mechX[k] = this.mechX[k + 1];
          this.mechY[k] = this.mechY[k + 1];
          this.mechTime[k] = this.mechTime[k + 1];
        }
        this.numMechs--;
      }
    }
  }

  // ============ CheckMech(Wiring.cs:313-327)============

  checkMech(i: number, j: number, time: number): boolean {
    for (let k = 0; k < this.numMechs; k++) {
      if (this.mechX[k] === i && this.mechY[k] === j) return false;
    }
    if (this.numMechs >= 999) return false;
    this.mechX[this.numMechs] = i;
    this.mechY[this.numMechs] = j;
    this.mechTime[this.numMechs] = time;
    this.numMechs++;
    return true;
  }

  // ============ HitSwitch(Wiring.cs:163-252)============

  hitSwitch(i: number, j: number): void {
    if (!this.st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
    const sh = this.sheetAt(i, j);
    const ti = this.p2i(i, j);
    // 压板/轨道/感应器/测重板/垫板/高尔夫(:167-171)与宝石锁(:172-176):直接 Trip
    if (sh === 135 || sh === 314 || sh === 423 || sh === 428 || sh === 442 || sh === 476) {
      this.tripWire(i, j, 1, 1);
      return;
    }
    if (sh === 440) { // 宝石锁 3×3
      this.tripWire(i, j, 3, 3);
      return;
    }
    if (sh === 136) { // 开关 frameY 0↔18(:177-182)
      this.st.setTile(i, j, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], this.st.frameY[ti] !== 0 ? 0 : 18);
      this.tripWire(i, j, 1, 1);
      return;
    }
    if (sh === 443) { // 热喷泉(:183-184)
      this.device?.(this, 443, i, j);
      return;
    }
    if (sh === 144) { // 计时器开/关(:185-196)
      if (this.st.frameY[ti] === 0) {
        this.st.setTile(i, j, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], 18);
        this.checkMech(i, j, 18000);
      } else {
        this.st.setTile(i, j, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], 0);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (sh === 441 || sh === 468) { // 陷阱箱 2×2(:197-208)
      const num1 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18) * -1;
      const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] / 18) * -1;
      let num3 = num1 % 4;
      if (num3 < -1) num3 += 2;
      this.tripWire(num3 + i, num2 + j, 2, 2);
      return;
    }
    if (sh === 467) { // 水晶陷阱箱(frameX/36==4 才触发,:209-222)
      if (Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 36) !== 4) return;
      const num1 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18) * -1;
      const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] / 18) * -1;
      let num3 = num1 % 4;
      if (num3 < -1) num3 += 2;
      this.tripWire(num3 + i, num2 + j, 2, 2);
      return;
    }
    if (sh === 132 || sh === 411) { // 拉杆/引爆器 2×2 翻帧(:223-251)
      let delta = 36;
      const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18) * -1;
      const num3 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] / 18) * -1;
      let num4 = num2 % 4;
      if (num4 < -1) { num4 += 2; delta = -36; }
      const ax = num4 + i, ay = num3 + j;
      if (this.sheetAt(ax, ay) === 411) this.checkMech(ax, ay, 60);
      for (let ix = ax; ix < ax + 2; ix++) {
        for (let iy = ay; iy < ay + 2; iy++) {
          if (!this.st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
          const ii = this.p2i(ix, iy);
          const s2 = this.sheetAt(ix, iy);
          if (s2 === 132 || s2 === 411) {
            this.st.setTile(ix, iy, this.st.type[ii], this.st.frameX[ii] + delta, this.st.frameY[ii]);
          }
        }
      }
      this.tripWire(ax, ay, 2, 2);
      return;
    }
  }
```

```
  tripWire(left: number, top: number, width: number, height: number): void {
    this.running = true;
    this.wireList.length = 0;
    this.wireDir.length = 0;
    const st = this.st;
    const pairs: Array<[[number, number], [number, number]]> = [];
    const bits = [TileStore.WIRE_RED, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW];
    // 四色 pass 顺序固定 红(1)蓝(2)绿(3)黄(4)(:405-508);每色前重置传送器对与泵
    for (let color = 1; color <= 4; color++) {
      this.tp[0] = [-1, -1]; this.tp[1] = [-1, -1];
      this.wireList.length = 0;
      const bit = bits[color - 1];
      for (let x = left; x < left + width; x++) {
        for (let y = top; y < top + height; y++) {
          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
          if (st.wire[this.p2i(x, y)] & bit) this.wireList.push(this.p2i(x, y));
        }
      }
      if (this.wireList.length > 0) {
        this.numInPump = 0;
        this.numOutPump = 0;
        this.hitWire(color);
        if (this.numInPump > 0 && this.numOutPump > 0) this.xferWater();
      }
      if (this.tp[0][0] >= 0 && this.tp[1][0] >= 0) pairs.push([[...this.tp[0]], [...this.tp[1]] as [number, number]]);
    }
    this.running = false;
    // 四色收集完的传送器对统一执行(:520-526)
    for (const [a, b] of pairs) this.teleportPair?.(a, b);
    this.pixelBoxPass();
    this.logicGatePass();
```

```
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Wiring } from '../src/world/Wiring';
import { deviceTable, type DeviceCtx } from '../src/world/wiring/devices';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';
import { TrapShot } from '../src/entities/Dart';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

// 炮弹投射物 1:1 回归（Wiring.cs case 209/1301-1341 + case 212/1345-1418 →
// WorldGen.ShootFromCannon WorldGen.cs:50606-50718；Projectile 162/281/166 AI 与 Kill）
type Shot = { px: number; py: number; vx: number; vy: number; damage: number; kind: string };

function makeCtx() {
  const shots: Shot[] = [];
  let bunnyCount = 0;
  const ctx: DeviceCtx = {
    dropItem: () => {}, spawnNpc: () => true, mechSpawnOk: () => true, itemSpawnOk: () => true,
    shootProjectile: (px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind) => shots.push({ px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind }),
    countProjectiles: (kind) => (kind === 'bunny' ? bunnyCount : 0),
    doorOpen: () => true, doorClose: () => true, teleportPair: () => {},
    relocateTownNpc: () => true, explode: () => {}, toast: () => {}, sfx: () => {},
    worldSurface: 100,
  };
  return { ctx, shots, setBunnyCount: (n: number) => { bunnyCount = n; } };
}

/** 放置一座 4×3 大炮（tile 209）：frameX = style*72 + col*18，frameY = angle*54 + row*18 */
function placeCannon(w: Wiring, ox: number, oy: number, style: number, angle: number) {
  const t = TILE_BY_KEY['v_209_cannon']!;
  for (let cx = 0; cx < 4; cx++) {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < 3; cy++) {
      w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, style * 72 + cx * 18, angle * 54 + cy * 18);
    }
  }
}

/** 放置一座 3×3 雪球发射器（tile 212）：frameX = facing*54 + col*18，frameY = row*18 */
function placeSnowLauncher(w: Wiring, ox: number, oy: number, facing: number) {
  const t = TILE_BY_KEY['v_212_snowballlauncher']!;
  for (let cx = 0; cx < 3; cx++) {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < 3; cy++) {
      w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, facing * 54 + cx * 18, cy * 18);
    }
  }
}

function freshWiring() {
  const world = new World(60, 60, 7, 'open');
  return new Wiring(world.store);
}
```

````
Design decisions:

### `src/world/Extractinator.ts`

```ts
// 提取机 1:1（ExtractinatorHelper.cs / ItemID.cs / Player.cs / Wiring.cs）
export type ExtractRng = { next(maxExclusive: number): number };  // hmm
```

For testability I'll accept an rng function. Let's define the roll function signature:

```ts
export interface ExtractinatorRng {
  /** Main.rand.Next(n) 语义：0..n-1 */
  nextInt(maxExclusive: number): number;
  /** Main.rand.Next(min, max) 语义：min..max-1 */
  nextRange(minInclusive: number, maxExclusive: number): number;
}
```

Or simpler: `rollExtractinatorDrop(mode, blockType, rng, hardMode)` where rng is `() => number` [0,1) and I compute. To keep exact integer semantics with a seeded RNG, better to accept an object with nextInt. For default use in Game, provide `randomExtractinatorRng()` using Math.random.

Hmm, simpler and exact: accept `rand: { next: (maxExclusive: number) => number }` and implement nextRange via next. All vanilla calls are Next(n) or Next(a,b) = a + Next(b-a).

Table data:
```ts
/** ItemID.Sets.ExtractinatorMode（ItemID.cs:1104）—— vanilla item id → 模式；未列入 = -1 */
export const EXTRACTINATOR_MODE: Readonly<Record<number, number>> = {
  424: 0, 1103: 0,          // SiltBlock / SlushBlock
  3347: 1,                  // DesertFossil
  2339: 2, 2338: 2, 2337: 2,// TinCan / FishingSeaweed / OldShoe
  4354: 3, 4389: 3, 4377: 3, 4378: 3, 5127: 3, 5128: 3, // 六种苔藓
  5395: 4,                  // PoopBlock
  1124: 5,                  // Hive
  4090: 6, 173: 6,          // ShellPileBlock / Obsidian
};
export function extractinatorMode(vanillaItemId: number): number { return EXTRACTINATOR_MODE[vanillaItemId] ?? -1; }

Careful: vanilla FindChest(i,j) finds a chest whose 2×2 area contains (i,j) — i.e., any of the chest's 4 tiles. Our ChestData stores only the anchor (x,y = top-left). To replicate FindChest over the scanned rectangle, for each candidate chest, check whether its 2×2 footprint intersects the scan box: `ch.x <= cx < ch.x+2`. Order: vanilla scans row-major from (ax-2, ay-2) and returns the first chest found at the scanned cell. Row-major order means smaller y first. Our world.chests order may differ. Let me implement: scan cells row-major (cy outer from ay-2 to ay+5, cx inner from ax-2 to ax+5), and for each cell find a chest containing it:
```ts
for (let cy = ay - 2; cy <= ay + 5; cy++) {
  for (let cx = ax - 2; cx <= ax + 5; cx++) {
    const c = this.world.chests.find((ch) => !ch.locked && cx >= ch.x && cx <= ch.x + 1 && cy >= ch.y && cy <= ch.y + 1);
    if (c) { chest = c; break outer; }
  }
}
```
That's exact 1:1 (FindChest at each cell). Perf: 8×8=64 cells × chests — fine for a wiring tick.

Math.max(val, 1) : 0;\n}\n\n// ============ 通电触发（Wiring.cs:2563-2622）============\n// 通电语义：提取机本身无库存——消耗源是锚点邻域内的箱子（非上方/内部方块）。\n// devices.ts 负责锚点回算 + checkMech(60) 冷却，箱子查找/消耗在这里（纯函数，可单测）。\n\n/** 箱子占位抽象（Game 侧 ChestData 的窄视图；items 的 id 为原版 item id） */\nexport interface ExtractChest {\n  /** 2×2 锚点（左上格） */\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  locked?: boolean;\n  items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>;\n}\n\n/**\n * TryFindChestForExtractinator（Wiring.cs:2596-2622）1:1：行扫描锚点邻域\n * (ax-2..ax+5)×(ay-2..ay+5)（:2603-2606 num=3/num2=3/num3=2），Chest.FindChest\n * 为 2×2 占位判定（命中占位内任意格都算）；上锁箱跳过（:2613）。\n */\nexport function findExtractinatorChest<C extends ExtractChest>(\n  chests: readonly C[], ax: number, ay: number,\n): C | undefined {\n  for (let cy = ay - 2; cy <= ay + 5; cy++) {\n    for (let cx = ax - 2; cx <= ax + 5; cx++) {\n      const c = chests.find((ch) =>\n        !ch.locked && cx >= ch.x && cx <= ch.x + 1 && cy >= ch.y && cy <= ch.y + 1);\n      if (c) return c;\n    }\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n/**\n * 箱内消耗（Wiring.cs:2575-2592）：items 自后向前（num3=maxItems-1..0）找首个非空格，\n * 每格只 roll 一次；itemType>0 才扣 1（:2584-2587）并停止，否则继续找下一格。\n */\nexport function extractinatorConsumeFromChest(\n  chest: ExtractChest,\n  blockType: number,\n  rand: ExtractinatorRand,\n  hardMode: boolean,\n): ExtractDrop | null {\n  for (let k = chest.items.length - 1; k >= 0; k--) {\n    const it = chest.items[k];\n    if (!it) continue;\n    const roll = rollExtractinatorDrop(extractinatorMode(it.id), blockType, rand, hardMode);\n    if (roll.itemType > 0) {\n      it.stack -= 1;\n      if (it.stack <= 0) chest.items[k] = null;\n      return roll;\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}"
}
````

```
Hmm, that write-back is convoluted and fragile. Much simpler: since `extractinatorConsumeFromChest` mutates the chest's items directly, I can pass a view whose `items` array aliases the real stacks. The only transformation needed is id → vanilla id, which is per-slot. I could instead make the pure function accept an `idOf` mapper... but that complicates the pure API.

Alternative cleaner approach: since `slotVanillaId` is a pure function of the item, and chests store internal ids, I can create a proxy items array of the same length where each entry is `{id: vanillaId, stack}` — but stack must alias the original number. Numbers aren't references in JS, so mutation of `it.stack` in the view won't propagate.

Options:
(a) Do the id mapping inside the loop in Game (duplicating 8 lines) — but then the pure function isn't used and isn't tested for the consume path... though it can still be tested with vanilla-id chests.
(b) Change `extractinatorConsumeFromChest` to accept `(chest, idToVanilla, ...)` — a mapper param keeps it pure and testable.
(c) Have the view items be objects wrapping the real slot objects: `{ get id() {return slotVanillaId(real.id)}, set stack(v){real.stack=v} }` — hacky.

Cleanest: (b) — pass an optional `idToVanilla?: (internalId: number) => number`. Then Game passes `slotVanillaId` and the function mutates the real chest items directly. Tests call it with chests holding vanilla ids and the identity mapper (default).

Let me do that.
```

````
function branchTable(mode: number, block: number) {
  let num = 5000, num2 = 25, num3 = 50, num4 = -1, num5 = -1, num6 = -1, num7 = 1, num8 = -1, num9 = -1, num10 = -1, num11 = -1;
  switch (mode) {

    case 2: num = -1; num2 = -1; num3 = -1; num4 = -1; num5 = 1; num7 = -1; break;
    case 3: num = -1; num2 = -1; num3 = -1; num4 = -1; num5 = -1; num7 = -1; num6 = 1; break;
    case 4: num = -1; num2 = -1; num3 = -1; num7 = -1; num9 = 50; num8 = 1; break;
    case 5: num = -1; num2 = -1; num3 = -1; num7 = -1; num11 = 1; break;
    case 6: num = -1; num2 = -1; num3 = -1; num7 = -1; num10 = 1; break;
  }
  return { num, num2, num3, num4, num5, num6, num7, num8, num9, num10, num11 };
}

function analyticCats(mode: number, block: number): Record<Cat, number> {
  if (mode < 0) return zero();
  const t = branchTable(mode, block);
  const p: Record<Cat, number> = zero();
  let rest = 1;
  const take = (prob: number): number => { const v = rest * prob; rest -= v; return v; };
  if (t.num4 !== -1) p.fossil = take(1 / t.num4);
  if (t.num7 !== -1) p.coinA = take(1 / 2);
  if (t.num !== -1) p.mosquito = take(1 / t.num);
  if (t.num5 !== -1) p.junk = take(1);
  if (t.num6 !== -1) {
    if (block === 642) { p.mossRare = take(1 / 10); p.mossCommon = take(1); }
    else p.mossCommon = take(1);
  }
  if (t.num9 !== -1) p.seeds = take(1 / t.num9);
  if (t.num8 > 0) p.dirt = take(1);
  if (t.num11 > 0) p.honey = take(1);
  if (t.num10 > 0) p.sand = take(1);
  if (t.num2 !== -1) p.gem = take(1 / t.num2);
  if (t.num3 !== -1) p.amber = take(1 / t.num3);
  p.coinB = take(1 / 3);
  p.ore = rest;
  return p;
}
```

   // 琥珀不是输入\n    expect(extractinatorMode(0)).toBe(-1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('转化表健全性', () => {\n  it('全配置分支概率和 = 100%（else-if 链穷尽，:72-487）', () => {\n    for (const c of CONFIGS) {\n      const p = analyticCats(c.mode, c.block);\n      const sum = Object.values(p).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);\n      expect(sum).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('mode -1（不可转化输入）恒返回 itemType -1', () => {\n    const d = rollExtractinatorDrop(-1, 219, seededRand(1), false);\n    expect(d.itemType).toBe(-1);\n    expect(d.stack).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  it('有效 mode 的 roll 永远产出已知产物且 stack ≥ 1', () => {\n    for (const c of CONFIGS) {\n      const rand = seededRand(c.mode * 100 + c.block + (c.hard ? 7 : 0));\n      for (let k = 0; k < 4000; k++) {\n        const d = rollExtractinatorDrop(c.mode, c.block, rand, c.hard);\n        expect(d.itemType, `mode=${c.mode} block=${c.block}`).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n        expect(CAT_OF[d.itemType], `未知产物 ${d.itemType}`).toBeDefined();\n        expect(d.stack).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('经验分布与解析概率一致（mode0 泥沙：钱币≈1/2 起步 + 兜底矿石）', () => {\n    const N = 60000;\n    const rand = seededRand(20260811);\n    const seen: Record<Cat, number> = analyticCats(0, 219);\n    for (const k of Object.keys(seen) as Cat[]) seen[k] = 0;\n    for (let k = 0; k < N; k++) {\n      const d = rollExtractinatorDrop(0, 219, rand, false);\n      seen[CAT_OF[d.itemType]!]!++;\n    }\n    const exp = analyticCats(0, 219);\n    for (const k of Object.keys(exp) as Cat[]) {\n      // 固定种子确定性采样；容差 0.012 ≈ 3.5σ（p=0.5, N=6e4）\n      expect(seen[k]! / N, `类别 ${k}`).toBeCloseTo(exp[k]!, 1.5);\n    }\n    // 关键锚点（wiki 口径）：钱币约 66.7%*、矿石约 30.8%*——此处只锁原版链的结构值\n    expect(exp.coin).toBeCloseTo(1 / 2 + (1 - 1 / 2) * (4999 / 5000) * (24 / 25) * (49 / 50) * (1 / 3), 9);\n    expect(exp.ore).toBeGreaterThan(0.25);\n    expect(exp.ore).toBeLessThan(0.35);\n  });\n\n  it('mode1 沙漠化石：坚固化石 1/10 + 琥珀蚊 1/1666（num/=3 整除，:25）', () => {\n    const exp = analyticCats(1, 219);\n    expect(exp.fossil).toBeCloseTo(0.1, 12);\n    // 1/2 钱币先扣，剩余再 1/1666\n    expect(exp.mosquito).toBeCloseTo((1 - 0.1) * 0.5 / 1666, 12);\n    expect(exp.amber).toBeCloseTo(0.9 * 0.5 * (1665 / 1666) * (49 / 50) / 20, 12);\n  });\n\n  it('产出 id 全部已注册（含 coin 体系与困难矿石）', () => {\n    const outputs = [3380, 71, 72, 73, 74, 1242, 2674, 2006, 2002, 2675,\n      4349, 4350, 4351, 4352, 4353, 4354, 4389, 4377, 5127, 4378,\n      62, 195, 194, 2, 1125, 169, 177, 178, 179, 180, 181, 182, 999,\n      12, 11, 14, 13, 699, 700, 701, 702, 364, 1104, 365, 1105, 366, 1106];\n    for (const vid of outputs) {\n      const key = COIN_KEY_BY_VID[vid] ?? VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];\n      expect(key, `产物 ${vid} 无 key`).toBeTruthy();\n      expect(ITEM_BY_KEY[key!], `产物 ${vid} 未注册`).toBeDefined();\n    }\n    // 输入也须已注册（手持/入箱才可能到达转化链）\n    for (const vid of Object.keys(EXTRACTINATOR_MODE).map(Number)) {\n      const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];\n      expect(key, `输入 ${vid} 无 key`).toBeTruthy();\n      expect(ITEM_BY_KEY[key!], `输入 ${vid} 未注册`).toBeDefined();\n    }\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('叶绿版（642）差异', () => {\n  it('使用间隔 ×0.33（Player.cs:41018-41020 + ApplyItemTime :4221-4233）', () => {\n    expect(extractinatorUseTime(10, 219)).toBe(10);\n    expect(extractinatorUseTime(10, 642)).toBe(3);   // (int)(10*0.33)=3\n    expect(extractinatorUseTime(1, 642)).toBe(1);    // Math.Max(val,1)\n    expect(extractinatorUseTime(2, 642)).toBe(1);    // (int)0.66=0 → 钳到 1\n  });\n\n  it('mode3 苔藓：普通版只出普通苔藓，叶绿版 1/10 改掷稀有池（:194-218）', () => {\n    const N = 20000;\n    const rare = new Set([4354, 4389, 4377, 5127, 4378]);\n    const common = new Set([4349, 4350, 4351, 4352, 4353]);\n    let rare642 = 0;\n    const rand219 = seededRand(31), rand642 = seededRand(32);\n    for (let k = 0; k < N; k++) {\n      const a = rollExtractinatorDrop(3, 219, rand219, false).itemType;\n      expect(common.has(a), `普通版出了稀有苔藓 ${a}`).toBe(true);\n      const b = rollExtractinatorDrop(3, 642, rand642, false).itemType;\n      if (rare.has(b)) rare642++;\n      else expect(common.has(b)).toBe(true);\n    }\n    // moss 分支内 rare 占 1/10；分支本身概率 1 → 总体 ≈ 0.1\n    expect(rare642 / N).toBeGreaterThan(0.09);\n    expect(rare642 / N).toBeLessThan(0.11);\n  });\n\n  it('兜底矿石：仅 642+困难模式用 14 种困难池（:463-466 / RollOreHardmode :490-509）', () => {\n    const prehmOnly = new Set([364, 1104, 365, 1105, 366, 1106]);\n    const hmSeen = new Set<number>();\n    const rand = seededRand(77);\n    for (let k = 0; k < 30000; k++) {\n      const a = rollExtractinatorDrop(0, 219, rand, true).itemType;   // 219 困难也走前困难池\n      expect(prehmOnly.has(a), `219 出了困难矿石 ${a}`).toBe(false);\n      const b = rollExtractinatorDrop(0, 642, rand, true).itemType;\n      if (CAT_OF[b] === 'ore') hmSeen.add(b);\n    }\n    for (const o of prehmOnly) expect(hmSeen.has(o), `642 困难池缺 ${o}`).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('矿物兑换表（ItemTrader.cs:82-108）', () => {\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[12]).toBe(699);   // 铜↔锡\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[699]).toBe(12);\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[364]).toBe(1104); // 钴↔钯\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[1106]).toBe(366); // 钛金↔精金\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[134]).toBe(137);  // 地牢砖循环 蓝→绿→粉→蓝\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[137]).toBe(139);\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[139]).toBe(134);\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[61]).toBe(3);     // 黑檀石→石头（FromAny）\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[370]).toBe(169);  // 黑檀沙→沙\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[833]).toBe(664);  // 紫冰→冰\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[3276]).toBe(3271);\n    expect(CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[999]).toBeUndefined(); // 非兑换物\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('通电触发（Wiring.cs:2563-2622）', () => {\n  function freshWiring() {\n    const world = new World(60, 60, 7, 'open');\n    return new Wiring(world.store);\n  }\n\n  /** 放置 3×3 提取机（frameX/frameY = 列/行 × 18，style 0） */\n  function placeExtractinator(w: Wiring, sheet: 219 | 642, ox: number, oy: number) {\n    const key = sheet === 219 ? 'v_219_extractinator' : 'v_642_chlorophyte_extractinator';\n    const t = TILE_BY_KEY[key]!;\n    for (let cx = 0; cx < 3; cx++) {\n      for (let cy = 0; cy < 3; cy++) {\n        w.st.setTileSilent(ox + cx, oy + cy, t, cx * 18, cy * 18);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  function makeCtx(chests: ExtractChest[] = []) {\n    const pulls: Array<[number, number, number]> = [];\n    const drops: Array<{ vid: number; stack: number; px: number; py: number }> = [];\n    const ctx: DeviceCtx = {\n      dropItem: () => {}, spawnNpc: () => true, mechSpawnOk: () => true, itemSpawnOk: () => true,\n      shootProjectile: () => {}, countProjectiles: () => 0,\n      doorOpen: () => true, doorClose: () => true, teleportPair: () => {},\n      relocateTownNpc: () => true, explode: () => {}, toast: () => {}, sfx: () => {},\n      extractinatorPull: (ax, ay, blockType) => {\n        pulls.push([ax, ay, blockType]);\n        const chest = findExtractinatorChest(chests, ax, ay);\n        if (!chest) return null;\n        return extractinatorConsumeFromChest(chest, blockType, seededRand(5), false);\n      },\n      dropItemStack: (vid, stack, px, py) => drops.push({ vid, stack, px, py }),\n      worldSurface: 30,\n    };\n    return { ctx, pulls, drops };\n  }\n\n  it('命中 3×3 任意格都回算到锚点（:2567-2568），且按 sheet 传机型', () => {\n    const w = freshWiring();\n    const { ctx, pulls } = makeCtx();\n    placeExtractinator(w, 219, 10, 20);\n    // 右下格 (12,22)：frameX=36 → 36%54/18=2 → 锚点 (10,20)\n    expect(deviceTable(w, ctx, 219, 12, 22)).toBe(true);\n    expect(pulls[0]).toEqual([10, 20, 219]);\n\n    const w2 = freshWiring();\n    const c2 = makeCtx();\n    placeExtractinator(w2, 642, 10, 20);\n    expect(deviceTable(w2, c2.ctx, 642, 10, 20)).toBe(true);\n    expect(c2.pulls[0]).toEqual([10, 20, 642]);\n  });\n\n  it('checkMech 60t 冷却内不重复触发（:2569/:2571）', () => {\n    const w = freshWiring();\n    const { ctx, pulls } = makeCtx();\n    placeExtractinator(w, 219, 10, 20);\n    expect(deviceTable(w, ctx, 219, 11, 21)).toBe(true);\n    expect(deviceTable(w, ctx, 219, 12, 22)).toBe(true); // 同一锚点被冷却拦截\n    expect(deviceTable(w, ctx, 219, 10, 20)).toBe(true);\n    expect(pulls).toHaveLength(1);\n  });\n\n  it('邻域箱子消耗 1 出 1 项：自后向前取首个可提取物（:2575-2592）', () => {\n    const chests: ExtractChest[] = [{\n      x: 10, y: 23, locked: false,   // 机器正下方（锚点 +3 行，扫描框内）\n      items: [{ id: 424, stack: 2 }, null, { id: 3347, stack: 1 }],\n    }];\n    const w = freshWiring();\n    const { ctx, pulls, drops } = makeCtx(chests);\n    placeExtractinator(w, 219, 10, 20);\n    expect(deviceTable(w, ctx, 219, 11, 21)).toBe(true);\n    expect(pulls).toHaveLength(1);\n    // 自后向前：首个非空是末格 3347（mode1），扣 1 后归零清格\n    expect(chests[0]!.items[2]).toBeNull();\n    expect(chests[0]!.items[0]).toEqual({ id: 424, stack: 2 });\n    expect(drops).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(drops[0]!.vid).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(drops[0]!.stack).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n    // 掉落位置 = 锚点 2×2 判定框中心（:2588 → ax*16+16, ay*16+16）\n    expect(drops[0]!.px).toBe(10 * 16 + 16);\n    expect(drops[0]!.py).toBe(20 * 16 + 16);\n  });\n\n  it('箱内无可转化物品 / 无箱 → 不掉落不消耗', () => {\n    const chests: ExtractChest[] = [{\n      x: 10, y: 23, locked: false,\n      items: [null, { id: 999, stack: 5 }],  // 琥珀是产物不是输入\n    }];\n    const w = freshWiring();\n    const { ctx, drops } = makeCtx(chests);\n    placeExtractinator(w, 219, 10, 20);\n    expect(deviceTable(w, ctx, 219, 11, 21)).toBe(true);\n    expect(drops).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(chests[0]!.items[1]).toEqual({ id: 999, stack: 5 }); // 未消耗\n\n    const w2 = freshWiring();\n    const c2 = makeCtx(); // 无箱\n    placeExtractinator(w2, 219, 10, 20);\n    expect(deviceTable(w2, c2.ctx, 219, 11, 21)).toBe(true);\n    expect(c2.drops).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('箱子查找（Wiring.cs:2596-2622 TryFindChestForExtractinator）', () => {\n  const mk = (x: number, y: number, locked = false): ExtractChest =>\n    ({ x, y, locked, items: [{ id: 424, stack: 9 }] });\n\n  it('扫描框 = 锚点 -2..+5（含端点，2×2 占位判定）', () => {\n    const ax = 20, ay = 20;\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([mk(ax, ay)], ax, ay)).toBeDefined();        // 锚点上\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([mk(ax + 5, ay + 5)], ax, ay)).toBeDefined(); // 远角含\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([mk(ax - 2, ay - 2)], ax, ay)).toBeDefined(); // 近角含\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([mk(ax + 6, ay)], ax, ay)).toBeUndefined();   // 越界\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([mk(ax - 3, ay)], ax, ay)).toBeUndefined();   // 越界\n    // 占位延伸：锚点在框外一格，但 2×2 占位伸进框内 → 命中（FindChest 语义）\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([mk(ax - 3, ay)], ax - 1, ay)).toBeDefined();\n  });\n\n  it('上锁箱跳过；行扫描序（先小 y）优先', () => {\n    const ax = 20, ay = 20;\n    const locked = mk(ax, ay, true), open = mk(ax + 1, ay);\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([locked, open], ax, ay)).toBe(open);\n    const below = mk(ax, ay + 1), right = mk(ax + 1, ay);\n    expect(findExtractinatorChest([below, right], ax, ay)).toBe(right); // y 小者先扫到\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('消耗语义（Wiring.cs:2584-2587）', () => {\n  it('自后向前、每格只 roll 一次、扣 1 清零归 null', () => {\n    const chest: ExtractChest = {\n      x: 0, y: 0, locked: false,\n      items: [{ id: 424, stack: 1 }, null, { id: 1103, stack: 3 }],\n    };\n    // 末格 1103（mode0）消耗 1 → 3→2\n    const d1 = extractinatorConsumeFromChest(chest, 219, seededRand(3), false);\n    expect(d1!.itemType).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(chest.items[2]).toEqual({ id: 1103, stack: 2 });\n    // 再来两次：2→1→清 null，然后落到首格 424\n    extractinatorConsumeFromChest(chest, 219, seededRand(4), false);\n    expect(chest.items[2]).toEqual({ id: 1103, stack: 1 });\n    extractinatorConsumeFromChest(chest, 219, seededRand(5), false);\n    expect(chest.items[2]).toBeNull();\n    const d4 = extractinatorConsumeFromChest(chest, 219, seededRand(6), false);\n    expect(d4!.itemType).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(chest.items[0]).toBeNull();\n    // 全空 → null\n    expect(extractinatorConsumeFromChest(chest, 219, seededRand(7), false)).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it('idToVanilla 映射下内部 id 也可消耗（Game 侧 slotVanillaId 桥接）', () => {\n    const INTERNAL_SILT = 9999; // 假内部 id，映射到原版 424\n    const chest: ExtractChest = { x: 0, y: 0, items: [{ id: INTERNAL_SILT, stack: 2 }] };\n    const d = extractinatorConsumeFromChest(chest, 219, seededRand(8), false, (id) =>\n      id === INTERNAL_SILT ? 424 : -1);\n    expect(d!.itemType).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(chest.items[0]).toEqual({ id: INTERNAL_SILT, stack: 1 });\n  });\n});\n"
}
````

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T13:37:29.225Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
My test expectation was wrong, not the code: chest at (ax-3, ay) occupies x = ax-3 and ax-2. The scan box includes cx = ax-2, and the chest's footprint covers (ax-2, ay) → found. So FindChest semantics DO match it. My assertion `toBeUndefined()` was incorrect. Vanilla FindChest(i,j) checks if any chest tile is at (i,j) — chest at (ax-3,ay) has tiles (ax-3,ay) and (ax-2,ay); scanning cell (ax-2, ay) hits it. So found is CORRECT 1:1 behavior.

Fix the test: (ax-3) with 2×2 footprint reaches ax-2 which is in the box → found. To test a true out-of-range chest, use (ax-4, ay) → footprint ax-4,ax-3, both outside [ax-2, ax+5] → undefined.
```

</details>
